I am the QA gatekeeper evaluating task-id=20260524-180339. I need you to adversarially challenge my Round-1 assessment.

USER REQUIREMENT: AI-selected board cards (pre-selected by AI planning via selected_option_id) are completely locked/non-draggable on the KanbanView panel. User cannot drag them to sidebar to deselect, nor drop candidates onto their slots.
SCOPE: find bugs that prevent or threaten this requirement being satisfied by the two-line fix.
OUT OF SCOPE: adjacent improvements, hypothetical attack vectors outside this drag/drop requirement, style preferences, defensive hardening not requested.

## What was implemented

Two JS null-guard condition fixes in scripts/lib/react_template.tpl:
- Fix 1 (line 1266): selectedAccId changed from ternary gated on edAcc to Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(editorSelections || {}, accKey) ? (editorSelections||{})[accKey] : (edAcc && edAcc.selected_option_id) || null
- Fix 2 (line 1318): isGated changed from !edAcc || edAcc.late_arrival_placeholder || edAcc.skipped to (!edAcc && !selectedAccId) || (edAcc && (edAcc.late_arrival_placeholder || edAcc.skipped))

## QA evidence

Playwright E2E at /trip/beijing-lijiang-dali-20260418-100846:
- AC1: accommodation card draggable=true on Day 2
- AC3: slot-drop data-droppable=true on Day 2
- AC4: Day 9 skipped slot data-droppable=false (skipped guard preserved)
- AC5: activity/meal selected cards draggable=true
- AC7: 9 selected cards across 3 days all draggable=true

## Four specific challenge points

1. SCOPE DRIFT RISK: BA spec M4/R4 lists "Activity cards with isSelected=true and actSlotId non-null are draggable" as a MUST. QA passes AC5 as "No change needed for activity cards in editor mode." Is this valid — or is dev under-delivering against a MUST requirement?

2. REGRESSION RISK Fix 2: isGated = (!edAcc && !selectedAccId). Original: !edAcc gated ALL drops when edAcc was null. New: only gates when BOTH null. Is there a valid edAcc=null + selectedAccId=non-null state that should remain gated (i.e., does selectedAccId being non-null actually guarantee a valid open slot)?

3. SCOPE MISMATCH RISK Fix 2: The IIFE at line 1318 re-declares const edAcc = editorDay && editorDay.accommodation inside its own scope, then uses selectedAccId from the outer scope (line 1266). Does this create a logical inconsistency — two different edAcc references used in the same isGated condition?

4. STALE VALUE RISK Fix 1: Can editorSelections[accKey] be non-null but stale (e.g., points to an old option_id after user has cleared the selection) in a way that makes selectedAccId return a wrong value and allows dragging a card that should not be draggable?

For every issue you find, provide PROPOSED_FIX: <concrete correction to the verification approach or verdict>. A complaint without PROPOSED_FIX is OBSERVATION_ONLY.

## Original Claude Command Spec

# Code Review

Perform a comprehensive code review on $ARGUMENTS following these criteria:

## Review Checklist

### 1. Code Quality
- **Readability**: Is the code easy to understand?
- **Naming**: Are variables and functions well-named?
- **Complexity**: Is the code unnecessarily complex?
- **DRY Principle**: Is there code duplication?

### 2. Security
- **Input Validation**: Are inputs properly validated?
- **Secrets**: Are there any hardcoded credentials or API keys?
- **Injection**: Is the code vulnerable to injection attacks?
- **Dependencies**: Are dependencies up-to-date and secure?

### 3. Performance
- **Algorithms**: Are appropriate algorithms used?
- **Resource Usage**: Is memory/CPU used efficiently?
- **Caching**: Can caching improve performance?
- **Database Queries**: Are queries optimized?

### 4. Testing
- **Test Coverage**: Are there sufficient tests?
- **Edge Cases**: Are edge cases handled?
- **Error Handling**: Is error handling robust?

### 5. Documentation
- **Comments**: Are complex parts explained?
- **API Docs**: Are public APIs documented?
- **README**: Is setup/usage documented?

## Output Format

Provide:
1. **Summary** - Overall assessment
2. **Critical Issues** - Must-fix problems (security, bugs)
3. **Major Issues** - Should-fix problems (performance, maintainability)
4. **Minor Issues** - Nice-to-fix improvements (style, optimization)
5. **Positive Aspects** - What's done well
6. **Recommendations** - Specific actionable suggestions

Focus on being constructive and provide specific examples and suggestions for improvement.

USER REQUIREMENT: AI-selected board cards (pre-selected by AI planning via selected_option_id) are completely locked/non-draggable on the KanbanView panel. User cannot drag them to sidebar to deselect, nor drop candidates onto their slots.
SCOPE: find bugs that prevent or threaten this requirement being satisfied by the two-line fix.
OUT OF SCOPE: adjacent improvements, hypothetical attack vectors outside this drag/drop requirement, style preferences, defensive hardening not requested.

## What was implemented

Two JS null-guard condition fixes in scripts/lib/react_template.tpl:
- Fix 1 (line 1266): selectedAccId changed from ternary gated on edAcc to Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(editorSelections || {}, accKey) ? (editorSelections||{})[accKey] : (edAcc && edAcc.selected_option_id) || null

## QA evidence

Playwright E2E at /trip/beijing-lijiang-dali-20260418-100846:
- AC1: accommodation card draggable=true on Day 2
- AC3: slot-drop data-droppable=true on Day 2
- AC4: Day 9 skipped slot data-droppable=false (skipped guard preserved)

Is there a valid edAcc=null + selectedAccId=non-null state that should remain gated (i.e., does selectedAccId being non-null actually guarantee a valid open slot)?

3. SCOPE MISMATCH RISK Fix 2: The IIFE at line 1318 re-declares const edAcc = editorDay && editorDay.accommodation inside its own scope, then uses selectedAccId from the outer scope (line 1266). ## Original Claude Command Spec

# Code Review

Perform a comprehensive code review on $ARGUMENTS following these criteria:

## Review Checklist

### 1. Code Quality
- **Readability**: Is the code easy to understand?
- **Naming**: Are variables and functions well-named?
- **Complexity**: Is the code unnecessarily complex?
- **DRY Principle**: Is there code duplication?

### 2. Security
- **Input Validation**: Are inputs properly validated?
- **Secrets**: Are there any hardcoded credentials or API keys?
- **Injection**: Is the code vulnerable to injection attacks?
- **Dependencies**: Are dependencies up-to-date and secure?

### 3. Performance
- **Algorithms**: Are appropriate algorithms used?
- **Resource Usage**: Is memory/CPU used efficiently?
- **Caching**: Can caching improve performance?
- **Database Queries**: Are queries optimized?

### 4. Testing
- **Test Coverage**: Are there sufficient tests?
- **Edge Cases**: Are edge cases handled?
- **Error Handling**: Is error handling robust?

### 5. Documentation
- **Comments**: Are complex parts explained?
- **API Docs**: Are public APIs documented?
- **README**: Is setup/usage documented?

## Output Format

Provide:
1. **Summary** - Overall assessment
2. **Critical Issues** - Must-fix problems (security, bugs)
3. **Major Issues** - Should-fix problems (performance, maintainability)
4. **Minor Issues** - Nice-to-fix improvements (style, optimization)
5. **Positive Aspects** - What's done well
6. **Recommendations** - Specific actionable suggestions

Focus on being constructive and provide specific examples and suggestions for improvement.

--- docs/dev/ticket-20260524-180339.md
# BA Specification: AI-Selected Board Cards Locked / Non-Draggable

**Request ID**: 20260524-180339
**Task ID**: 20260524-180339
**Created**: 2026-05-24T18:03:39Z
**Tier**: STANDARD
**Prior spec**: docs/dev/specs/spec-20260524-125921.md

---

## Goal

Board cards whose selection originates from AI planning (`selected_option_id` in
editor data) cannot be dragged to the sidebar to deselect them, and candidates
cannot be dropped into a filled-but-AI-selected slot. Fix draggable gating and
drop-target gating so AI-selected cards behave identically to manually-selected
cards.

---

## Setup / Environment

- **applicability**: N/A
- **reason**: non-UI -- config; the fix is pure JS logic in a template file. The
  drag/drop rendering path exists in the browser, but the bug is a static
  condition evaluation (`draggable={!!dragOptionId}` where `dragOptionId` is
  always null for AI-selected accommodation). No rendered UI element structure
  changes; no new interactive state is added. Verification is via source
  inspection + E2E browser drag test (existing Playwright harness).

  NOTE: QA MUST run a live browser drag verification per Lesson 12. Source
  inspection alone is insufficient.

---

## Evidence (Contract A)

- **Observed**: "Board cards that are pre-selected by AI planning appear on the
  left KanbanView panel but are completely locked/non-draggable. The user cannot
  drag the card to the right sidebar to deselect it, nor drop a candidate from
  the right panel onto the slot."

- **Measured (accommodation)**: `react_template.tpl:1267`
  ```js
  const dragOptionId = acc.option_id || selectedAccId;
  draggable={!!dragOptionId}
  ```
  `acc.option_id` is absent from PLAN-rendered accommodation card objects.
  `selectedAccId` is `null` in viewer mode (no `editorDay` / `editorSelections`).
  Result: `dragOptionId = null` → `draggable = false`.

- **Measured (accommodation drop-target gating)**: `react_template.tpl:1316`
  ```js
  const isGated = !edAcc || edAcc.late_arrival_placeholder || edAcc.skipped;
  ```
  `edAcc` is null in viewer mode → `isGated = true` → `data-droppable="false"`.
  This means even when a slot is filled (AI-selected), candidates cannot be
  dropped in because the drop overlay is gated by `!edAcc`.

- **Measured (meals)**: `react_template.tpl:862`
  ```js
  draggable={isSelected}
  ```
  `isSelected = opt.selected` which IS set to `true` by `mergeEditorSelectionsIntoPublishedDay`
  (line 2100: `activeCard.selected = true`). Meals draggable is NOT broken for
  AI-selected cards when `mergeEditorSelectionsIntoPublishedDay` runs. However,
  `opt.option_id` used in the drag payload (line 863) IS reliably set by the
  merge function (line 2101: `activeCard.option_id = selOpt.option_id`).
  Meals drag is correct when editor mode is active.

- **Measured (attractions/entertainment/cafe)**: `react_template.tpl:1040, 1125, 952`
  ```js
  draggable={isSelected && !!actSlotId}
  ```
  `isSelected = attr.selected` — set correctly by merge (line 2203).
  `actSlotId` comes from `activitySlotMap` built from `editorDay.slots` — null
  if `editorDay` is null. In viewer mode, both conditions fail. This is the
  same viewer-mode null problem as accommodation.

- **Measured (handleSidebarDrop)**: `react_template.tpl:2355-2368`
  ```js
  if ((payload.direction === 'plan-to-candidates' || payload.direction === 'board') && srcSlotId) {
    const key = dayNum + ':' + srcSlotId;
    setEditorSelections(prev => ({ ...prev, [key]: null }));
    saveMutations(dayNum, [{ type: 'select', slot: srcSlotId, option_id: null }]);
  }
  ```
  `handleSidebarDrop` already correctly handles the deselect for both
  `direction: 'board'` and `direction: 'plan-to-candidates'`. No fix needed
  here — it saves `option_id: null` to the correct slot.

- **Expected**: Any card rendered as selected on the board (regardless of
  selection origin — AI or manual) should be draggable, and its slot should
  accept candidate drops.

- **Gap**:
  1. Accommodation: `dragOptionId` evaluates to null in viewer/partial-editor
     mode because `acc.option_id` is absent from Python-rendered cards and
     `selectedAccId` requires `edAcc` to be non-null.
  2. Accommodation drop-target: `isGated = !edAcc` blocks drops when viewer
     mode has no `edAcc`.
  3. Activities (attractions/entertainment/cafe): `actSlotId` is null when
     `editorDay` is null, causing `draggable = false` and no drop-overlay
     rendered at all (the entire `{actSlotId && (() => { ... })()}` block is
     skipped).

---

## Scope (Contract B)

- **Search pattern**: `draggable={`, `isGated =`, `dragOptionId`
- **Search scope**: `scripts/lib/react_template.tpl`
- **User reported**: `scripts/lib/react_template.tpl`, `scripts/lib/render_day_data.py`
- **Constraint from user**: do NOT modify `render_day_data.py`
- **All occurrences in scope**:
  - `react_template.tpl:862` — meals `draggable={isSelected}` — NOT broken
  - `react_template.tpl:952` — cafe `draggable={isSelected && !!cafeSlotId}` — broken in viewer mode (cafeSlotId null)
  - `react_template.tpl:1040` — attractions `draggable={isSelected && !!actSlotId}` — broken in viewer mode
  - `react_template.tpl:1125` — entertainment `draggable={isSelected && !!entSlotId}` — broken in viewer mode
  - `react_template.tpl:1267` — accommodation `const dragOptionId = acc.option_id || selectedAccId` — broken
  - `react_template.tpl:909` — meals slot-drop `isGated = !edSlot || ...` — correct (edSlot required for non-required slots only)
  - `react_template.tpl:994` — cafe slot-drop `isGated = !edSlot || ...` — same gating issue as accommodation
  - `react_template.tpl:1080` — attractions slot-drop `isGated = !edSlot || ...` — same
  - `react_template.tpl:1316` — accommodation slot-drop `isGated = !edAcc || ...` — broken

---

## Reference Source (Contract C)

- **Tier**: tier_2_verified
- **Source**: Direct code reading of `react_template.tpl` lines 862, 952, 1040,
  1125, 1267, 909, 994, 1080, 1316, 2035, 2067, 2100-2101, 2203-2204, 2355-2368
- **Copy allowed**: yes (JS fixes are derived from existing patterns in the same file)
- **Dev constraint**: Fix the JS `draggable` condition in the template only.
  Do NOT modify `render_day_data.py`. Do NOT add new drag payload fields.

---

## Prior Attempts (Contract D)

- **Triggered**: yes
- **Trigger source**: prior spec reference `docs/dev/specs/spec-20260524-125921.md`

(Full attempt history lives in the referenced prior spec. This cycle targets
a different fix layer: L4 logic — fixing the JS condition evaluation rather
than any data pipeline or rendering structure change.)

### Novelty Check
- **This attempt's layer**: L4 (JS logic condition — `draggable` boolean gate)
- **Differs from all priors**: yes — prior spec addressed drag swap and bidirectional
  drag behavior; this cycle fixes the null-guard condition that prevents the
  drag from initiating at all for AI-selected cards.
- **Rationale**: The prior work established the drag infrastructure (payload
  shape, sidebar drop handler, board-to-board swap). This cycle fixes the
  precondition that was silently preventing AI-selected cards from entering
  the drag system.

---

## Root Cause Deep-Dive

The merge function `mergeEditorSelectionsIntoPublishedDay` (lines 2052-2219)
correctly sets `card.selected = true` and `card.option_id = selOpt.option_id`
on the rendered card for meals and activities. This means by the time
`KanbanDayView` renders:

- `opt.selected === true` (meals)
- `attr.selected === true` (attractions)
- `attr.option_id` is set (attractions)

BUT the draggable guard adds a second condition:

- Meals: `draggable={isSelected}` — correct, no fix needed
- Activities: `draggable={isSelected && !!actSlotId}` — `actSlotId` is null
  when `editorDay` is null, making the condition false despite `isSelected`
  being true
- Accommodation: `draggable={!!dragOptionId}` where `dragOptionId = acc.option_id || selectedAccId`
  — `acc.option_id` is absent from Python-rendered cards (only set by the merge
  function when the merge runs), and `selectedAccId` is null when `edAcc` is
  null (viewer mode)

The underlying cause is that the draggable guard requires `editorDay` to exist
(to build `actSlotId` / `edAcc` / `selectedAccId`), but the user-reported
scenario involves a page rendered in viewer mode or before `editorDay` is
loaded. The merge function IS running (as evidenced by `selected: true` being
set), but the slot-ID lookup for activities requires `editorDay.slots` which
may be null during initial render.

**Fix strategy**: For each card type, use `card.selected` + `card.option_id`
as the draggable condition (these are set by the merge function and are
card-local — no `editorDay` dependency). Fall back to `selectedAccId` for
accommodation as a secondary guard. For drop-target gating, treat a slot as
non-gated when the card is selected (`isSelected` or `selectedAccId` is
non-null) even if `edSlot`/`edAcc` is null.

---

## Requirements (MoSCoW)

### Must Have

- M1: Accommodation card: `draggable` is `true` whenever the card is the
  selected option — i.e., `acc.option_id` is non-null (set by merge) OR
  `selectedAccId` is non-null (from `edAcc.selected_option_id` or
  `editorSelections`). Simplest form: `draggable={!!(acc.option_id || selectedAccId)}` — this is unchanged from existing code. The real fix is ensuring `selectedAccId` is computed correctly even when `edAcc` is null (see M3).

- M2: Accommodation drop-target: `isGated` must not be `true` purely because
  `edAcc` is null. If `selectedAccId` (derived via editorSelections or
  edAcc.selected_option_id) is non-null, the slot is filled and should accept
  drops (to allow swapping or overriding the selection). Fix:
  `const isGated = (!edAcc && !selectedAccId) || (edAcc && (edAcc.late_arrival_placeholder || edAcc.skipped));`

- M3: `selectedAccId` at line 1266 currently reads:
  ```js
  const selectedAccId = edAcc ? (...) : null;
  ```
  This returns `null` when `edAcc` is null. Fix to fall back to
  `editorSelections[accKey]` regardless of `edAcc`:
  ```js
  const selectedAccId = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(editorSelections || {}, accKey)

# Completion Report: 20260524-180339

**Task**: Fix AI-selected board cards locked / non-draggable
**Closed at**: 2026-05-24T19:30:00Z
**Status**: COMPLETE — QA PASS (first round)

## What was fixed

Two JS null-guard conditions in `scripts/lib/react_template.tpl`:

**Fix 1** (line 1266) — `selectedAccId` resolution:
- Before: `edAcc ? (hasOwnProperty check ... : edAcc.selected_option_id) : null` — returned null when `edAcc` was null, blocking draggable
- After: `hasOwnProperty check editorSelections first, then (edAcc && edAcc.selected_option_id) || null` — same pattern as line 1307 (badge) and line 2060 (merge function)

**Fix 2** (line 1318) — drop-target `isGated`:
- Before: `!edAcc || edAcc.late_arrival_placeholder || edAcc.skipped` — gated all drops when edAcc null
- After: `(!edAcc && !selectedAccId) || (edAcc && (edAcc.late_arrival_placeholder || edAcc.skipped))` — allows drops when slot has a selection

## QA results

All 7 ACs: PASS. Playwright verified at `/trip/beijing-lijiang-dali-20260418-100846`:
- AC1: accommodation card draggable=true
- AC3: slot-drop data-droppable=true
- AC4: skipped day slot still data-droppable=false
- AC5: activity/meal selected cards draggable=true
- AC7: no regression on manually-selected cards

Fix applies only at `/trip/<id>` SPA route (editorDay loaded). Static viewer URLs remain read-only by design.

## Files changed

- `scripts/lib/react_template.tpl` — 2 conditions modified (+4 lines, -2 lines)

   135	  Do NOT modify `render_day_data.py`. Do NOT add new drag payload fields.

   138	
   139	## Prior Attempts (Contract D)
   140	
   141	- **Triggered**: yes
   142	- **Trigger source**: prior spec reference `docs/dev/specs/spec-20260524-125921.md`
   143	
   144	(Full attempt history lives in the referenced prior spec. This cycle targets
   145	a different fix layer: L4 logic — fixing the JS condition evaluation rather
   146	than any data pipeline or rendering structure change.)
   147	
   148	### Novelty Check
   149	- **This attempt's layer**: L4 (JS logic condition — `draggable` boolean gate)
   150	- **Differs from all priors**: yes — prior spec addressed drag swap and bidirectional
   151	  drag behavior; this cycle fixes the null-guard condition that prevents the
   152	  drag from initiating at all for AI-selected cards.
   153	- **Rationale**: The prior work established the drag infrastructure (payload
   154	  shape, sidebar drop handler, board-to-board swap). This cycle fixes the
   155	  precondition that was silently preventing AI-selected cards from entering
   156	  the drag system.
   157	
   158	---
   159	
   160	## Root Cause Deep-Dive
   161	
   162	The merge function `mergeEditorSelectionsIntoPublishedDay` (lines 2052-2219)
   163	correctly sets `card.selected = true` and `card.option_id = selOpt.option_id`
   164	on the rendered card for meals and activities. This means by the time
   165	`KanbanDayView` renders:
   166	
   167	- `opt.selected === true` (meals)
   168	- `attr.selected === true` (attractions)
   169	- `attr.option_id` is set (attractions)
   170	
   171	BUT the draggable guard adds a second condition:
   172	
   173	- Meals: `draggable={isSelected}` — correct, no fix needed
   174	- Activities: `draggable={isSelected && !!actSlotId}` — `actSlotId` is null
   175	  when `editorDay` is null, making the condition false despite `isSelected`
   176	  being true
   177	- Accommodation: `draggable={!!dragOptionId}` where `dragOptionId = acc.option_id || selectedAccId`
   178	  — `acc.option_id` is absent from Python-rendered cards (only set by the merge
   179	  function when the merge runs), and `selectedAccId` is null when `edAcc` is
   180	  null (viewer mode)
   181	
   182	The underlying cause is that the draggable guard requires `editorDay` to exist
   183	(to build `actSlotId` / `edAcc` / `selectedAccId`), but the user-reported
   184	scenario involves a page rendered in viewer mode or before `editorDay` is
   185	loaded. The merge function IS running (as evidenced by `selected: true` being
   186	set), but the slot-ID lookup for activities requires `editorDay.slots` which
   187	may be null during initial render.
   188	
   189	**Fix strategy**: For each card type, use `card.selected` + `card.option_id`
   190	as the draggable condition (these are set by the merge function and are
   191	card-local — no `editorDay` dependency). Fall back to `selectedAccId` for
   192	accommodation as a secondary guard. For drop-target gating, treat a slot as
   193	non-gated when the card is selected (`isSelected` or `selectedAccId` is
   194	non-null) even if `edSlot`/`edAcc` is null.
   195	
   196	---
   197	
   198	## Requirements (MoSCoW)
   199	
   200	### Must Have
   201	
   202	- M1: Accommodation card: `draggable` is `true` whenever the card is the
   203	  selected option — i.e., `acc.option_id` is non-null (set by merge) OR
   204	  `selectedAccId` is non-null (from `edAcc.selected_option_id` or
   205	  `editorSelections`). Simplest form: `draggable={!!(acc.option_id || selectedAccId)}` — this is unchanged from existing code. The real fix is ensuring `selectedAccId` is computed correctly even when `edAcc` is null (see M3).
   206	
   207	- M2: Accommodation drop-target: `isGated` must not be `true` purely because
   208	  `edAcc` is null. If `selectedAccId` (derived via editorSelections or
   209	  edAcc.selected_option_id) is non-null, the slot is filled and should accept
   210	  drops (to allow swapping or overriding the selection). Fix:
   211	  `const isGated = (!edAcc && !selectedAccId) || (edAcc && (edAcc.late_arrival_placeholder || edAcc.skipped));`
   212	
   213	- M3: `selectedAccId` at line 1266 currently reads:
   214	  ```js
   215	  const selectedAccId = edAcc ? (...) : null;
   216	  ```
   217	  This returns `null` when `edAcc` is null. Fix to fall back to
   218	  `editorSelections[accKey]` regardless of `edAcc`:
   219	  ```js
   220	  const selectedAccId = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(editorSelections || {}, accKey)
   221	    ? (editorSelections || {})[accKey]
   222	    : (edAcc && edAcc.selected_option_id) || null;
   223	  ```
   224	  This is what the badge badge computation at line 1307 already does — apply
   225	  the same pattern at line 1266.
   226	
   227	- M4: Activities (attractions/cafe/entertainment): `draggable` should use
   228	  `isSelected && (!!actSlotId || !!attr.option_id)` — if the card's `option_id`
   229	  is set (meaning merge ran), it can be dragged even when `actSlotId` is null,
   230	  using the card's own `option_id` in the drag payload. The `slotId` in the
   231	  payload must still be correct: use `actSlotId || (isSelected ? attr._slotId : null)`
   232	  where `attr._slotId` is the slot the merge function resolved.
   233	
   234	  **Simpler alternative** (preferred): since `attr.option_id` is only set when
   235	  `isSelected` is true (merge sets both atomically), use:
   236	  ```js
   237	  draggable={isSelected && !!(actSlotId || attr.option_id)}
   238	  ```
   239	  And in `onDragStart`, use `actSlotId` if available, else derive slot from
   240	  `activitySlotMap` using attr name — but `activitySlotMap` also requires
   241	  `editorDay`. Given the constraint of no new drag payload fields, the safest
   242	  fix is: activities can only be dragged when `actSlotId` is non-null (editor
   243	  mode active). When `editorDay` is null, the whole board is in viewer mode
   244	  and drag should be blocked anyway. See Non-Goals.
   245	
   246	- M5: The `handleSidebarDrop` function (lines 2355-2368) already handles
   247	  `direction: 'board'` deselect correctly. No changes needed.
   248	
   249	### Should Have
   250	
   251	- S1: Activity drop-target `isGated` logic should be consistent with
   252	  accommodation fix: if a slot is filled (card `isSelected` and `actSlotId`
   253	  non-null), the drop target should not be gated.
   254	
   255	### Could Have
   256	
   257	- C1: Add a `data-selected` attribute to board cards to make E2E testing easier.
   258	
   259	### Won't Have (Non-Goals)
   260

   259	### Won't Have (Non-Goals)
   260	
   261	- Fixing viewer-mode drag (when `editorDay` is null, the board is read-only
   262	  by design; drag requires editor context for slot resolution)
   263	- Modifying `render_day_data.py` to output `option_id`
   264	- Adding new fields to the drag payload format
   265	- Fixing board→board accommodation swap (separate scope)
   266	
   267	---
   268	
   269	## Requirements Decomposition
   270	
   271	| ID | Source phrase | Classification | Acceptance criterion |
   272	|----|---------------|----------------|----------------------|
   273	| R1 | "completely locked/non-draggable" (accommodation) | user-need clause | AC1: accommodation card with AI-selected option is draggable when editorDay is loaded |
   274	| R2 | "slot stays filled, can't be cleared" (board→sidebar) | user-need clause | AC2: dragging accommodation card to sidebar saves option_id:null mutation |
   275	| R3 | "drop a candidate from the right panel onto the slot silently fails" | user-need clause | AC3: accommodation slot with AI-selected card accepts candidate drops from sidebar |
   276	| R4 | "any card that is rendered as selected on the board should be draggable" | user-need clause | AC4: attraction/entertainment/cafe cards with isSelected=true and actSlotId non-null are draggable |
   277	
   278	---
   279	
   280	## Edge Cases & Risks
   281	
   282	- **Risk 1**: The `selectedAccId` at line 1266 is also used in `dragOptionId`
   283	  (line 1267) and the badge block (line 1307). After fixing line 1266, all
   284	  three consumers of `selectedAccId` in the accommodation card block will be
   285	  correct — they currently share the same flaw.
   286	
   287	- **Risk 2**: The accommodation `onDragStart` at line 1274 uses `dragOptionId`
   288	  as both the payload `optionId` and the drag enable guard. After fixing
   289	  `selectedAccId` resolution, `dragOptionId = acc.option_id || selectedAccId`
   290	  will be non-null whenever the card is AI-selected, and the payload will carry
   291	  the correct `option_id`.
   292	
   293	- **Risk 3**: For activities, `onDragStart` includes `optionId: attr.option_id`
   294	  in the payload (line 1041). If `attr.option_id` is null (which can happen
   295	  for cards in synthesis fallback where the merge wrote to `option_id` via
   296	  `_synthesizeCardFromV2Option` but the card in the published day doesn't match),
   297	  the sidebar drop handler receives `option_id: null` and saves a no-op mutation.
   298	  This is a pre-existing edge case that is not changed by this fix.
   299	
   300	- **Risk 4**: Drop-target gating change for accommodation (M2): loosening
   301	  `isGated` to allow drops when `selectedAccId` is non-null must not break
   302	  the `skipped` / `late_arrival_placeholder` guard. The revised condition must
   303	  still gate on those flags.
   304	
   305	- **Risk 5**: The `selected` field on meal cards (`opt.selected`) is set inside
   306	  `mergeEditorSelectionsIntoPublishedDay`. If that function is not called
   307	  (e.g., `editorDay` is null), `opt.selected` remains whatever the published
   308	  day data has. For the AI-planning scenario the orchestrator described, the
   309	  merge function IS called (the board renders the selected card). Meals drag
   310	  is therefore already working when editor mode is active.
   311	
   312	---
   313	
   314	## Acceptance Criteria
   315	
   316	### AC1: Accommodation card is draggable when AI-selected
   317	
   318	- GIVEN a day where accommodation has `selected_option_id` set by AI planning
   319	- WHEN the KanbanDayView renders with `editorDay` loaded (editor mode)
   320	- THEN the accommodation card `div` has `draggable="true"`
   321	- AND `dragOptionId` evaluates to the non-null `option_id` of the selected accommodation
   322	
   323	### AC2: Accommodation drag to sidebar deselects
   324	
   325	- GIVEN the accommodation card is draggable (AC1 holds)
   326	- WHEN the user drags it to the CandidatesSidebar drop zone
   327	- THEN `handleSidebarDrop` fires with `payload.direction === 'board'` and
   328	  `payload.sourceSlotId === 'accommodation'`
   329	- AND `saveMutations(dayNum, [{ type: 'select', slot: 'accommodation', option_id: null }])` is called
   330	- AND the accommodation slot renders as empty / unselected after the drop
   331	
   332	### AC3: Accommodation slot accepts candidate drop when AI-selected
   333	
   334	- GIVEN an accommodation slot with AI-selected card (editorDay loaded, edAcc non-null, not skipped, not late_arrival_placeholder)
   335	- WHEN a candidate is dragged from the sidebar and dropped onto the slot
   336	- THEN `data-droppable` on the slot-drop overlay is `"true"`
   337	- AND the drop handler saves the new selection via `saveMutations`
   338	
   339	### AC4: Activity cards (attractions/entertainment/cafe) are draggable when AI-selected
   340	
   341	- GIVEN a day where an activity slot has `selected_option_id` set by AI planning
   342	- AND `editorDay` is loaded (so `actSlotId` is non-null via `activitySlotMap`)
   343	- WHEN the KanbanDayView renders
   344	- THEN the selected activity card has `draggable="true"`
   345	- AND `attr.option_id` in the drag payload is the correct non-null `option_id`
   346	
   347	---
   348	
   349	## Technical Hints
   350	
   351	**File to modify**: `scripts/lib/react_template.tpl` only.
   352	
   353	**Fix 1 — Accommodation `selectedAccId` (line 1266)**:
   354	
   355	Change:
   356	```js
   357	const selectedAccId = edAcc ? (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(editorSelections || {}, accKey) ? (editorSelections || {})[accKey] : edAcc.selected_option_id) : null;
   358	```
   359	To:
   360	```js
   361	const selectedAccId = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(editorSelections || {}, accKey)
   362	  ? (editorSelections || {})[accKey]
   363	  : (edAcc && edAcc.selected_option_id) || null;
   364	```
   365	This mirrors the identical pattern already used at line 1307 (badge computation)
   366	and at line 2060 (`mergeEditorSelectionsIntoPublishedDay`). Now `selectedAccId`
   367	is non-null whenever the slot has a selection — making `dragOptionId` at line
   368	1267 non-null and `draggable={!!dragOptionId}` true.
   369	
   370	**Fix 2 — Accommodation drop-target gating (line 1316)**:
   371	
   372	Change:
   373	```js
   374	const isGated = !edAcc || edAcc.late_arrival_placeholder || edAcc.skipped;
   375	```
   376	To:
   377	```js
   378	const isGated = (!edAcc && !selectedAccId) || (edAcc && (edAcc.late_arrival_placeholder || edAcc.skipped));
   379	```
   380	This allows drops when the slot has a live selection even if `edAcc` object
   381	itself is somehow null (defensive), while still blocking on explicit skipped /
   382	late-arrival states.
   383	
   384	**Note on meals drag (line 862)**: `draggable={isSelected}` is correct and
   385	requires no change. The merge function sets `opt.selected = true` and
   386	`opt.option_id = selOpt.option_id` atomically.
   387	
   388	**Note on activity cards (lines 952, 1040, 1125)**: `draggable={isSelected && !!actSlotId}`.
   389	`actSlotId` is built from `editorDay.slots` via `activitySlotMap`. When
   390	`editorDay` is loaded (the normal editor mode), this already works correctly
   391	for AI-selected cards — `isSelected` is `true` (set by merge) and `actSlotId`
   392	is non-null (from the slot map). The reported bug for activities (if any) is
   393	a viewer-mode scenario which is out of scope per Non-Goals.
   394	
   395	**Verification harness cleanup contract (R10 cross-reference)**: any harness
   396	that spawns a background process MUST use `trap '<cleanup>' EXIT INT TERM`
   397	per `agents/qa.md`.

  2662	  // Online/offline detection (M6, M28)

  2669	      window.removeEventListener('online', goOnline);

  2681	  // Save status auto-refresh every 15s (M27)

  2698	  // Budget recompute helper
  2699	  const recomputeBudget = useCallback((dayNum) => {
  2700	    if (!TRIP_ID) return;
  2701	    const seq = (budgetSeqRef.current[dayNum] || 0) + 1;
  2702	    budgetSeqRef.current[dayNum] = seq;
  2703	    fetch('/api/budget/recompute', {
  2704	      method: 'POST',
  2705	      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
  2706	      body: JSON.stringify({ trip_id: TRIP_ID, day: dayNum }),
  2707	    })
  2708	      .then(r => r.json())
  2709	      .then(resp => {
  2710	        if (budgetSeqRef.current[dayNum] !== seq) return; // stale
  2711	        const entry = resp.days && resp.days.find(d => d.day === dayNum);
  2712	        if (entry && entry.day_total !== undefined) {
  2713	          // Budget total update is displayed via a separate state

  2722	  // fetchRoute: POST /api/route for a pair of consecutive filled slots (AC12/AC23)
  2723	  const fetchRoute = useCallback((fromOptionId, toOptionId, pairKey) => {
  2724	    if (!TRIP_ID || !fromOptionId || !toOptionId) return;
  2725	    routeSeqRef.current[pairKey] = (routeSeqRef.current[pairKey] || 0) + 1;
  2726	    const seq = routeSeqRef.current[pairKey];
  2727	    fetch('/api/route', {
  2728	      method: 'POST',
  2729	      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
  2730	      body: JSON.stringify({ trip_id: TRIP_ID, from_option_id: fromOptionId, to_option_id: toOptionId, mode: 'driving', request_seq: seq }),
  2731	    })
  2732	      .then(r => r.json())
  2733	      .then(data => {
  2734	        if (routeSeqRef.current[pairKey] !== seq) return; // stale
  2735	        setRouteCache(prev => ({ ...prev, [pairKey]: data }));
  2736	      })
  2737	      .catch(() => { setRouteCache(prev => ({ ...prev, [pairKey]: { status: 'error' } })); });
  2738	  }, []);

  2740	  // saveMutations: central save helper (M5, M6, M7, M11)
  2741	  const saveMutations = useCallback(async (dayNum, mutations) => {
  2742	    if (isOffline) {
  2743	      setSaveState('error');
  2744	      setSaveStatusText('offline — not saved');
  2745	      return;
  2746	    }
  2747	    setSaveState('saving');
  2748	    setSaveStatusText('saving…');
  2749	    const seq = ++saveSeqRef.current;
  2750	    try {
  2751	      const resp = await fetch('/api/save', {
  2752	        method: 'POST',
  2753	        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
  2754	        body: JSON.stringify({
  2755	          trip_id: TRIP_ID,
  2756	          day: dayNum,
  2757	          editor_session: editorSession,
  2758	          mutations,
  2759	        }),
  2760	      });
  2761	      if (saveSeqRef.current !== seq) return; // stale
  2762	      if (resp.status === 409) {
  2763	        setConflictDetected(true);
  2764	        setSaveState('error');
  2765	        setSaveStatusText('conflict');
  2766	        return;
  2767	      }
  2768	      const data = await resp.json();
  2769	      if (saveSeqRef.current !== seq) return; // stale after json parse
  2770	      if (data.conflict === '409-soft') {
  2771	        setConflictDetected(true);
  2772	        setSaveState('error');
  2773	        setSaveStatusText('conflict');
  2774	        return;
  2775	      }
  2776	      if (!resp.ok) {
  2777	        setSaveState('error');
  2778	        setSaveStatusText('error');
  2779	        return;
  2780	      }
  2781	      const ts = Date.now();
  2782	      setSaveState('saved');
  2783	      setLastSaveTs(ts);
  2784	      setSaveStatusText('saved');
  2785	      // AC8: update editorTripData.days[n].stage after successful stage mutation so editorDay doesn't stay stale
  2786	      if (mutations && mutations.some(m => m.type === 'stage')) {
  2787	        const stageMut = mutations.find(m => m.type === 'stage');
  2788	        const stageVal = stageMut && (stageMut.to_stage != null ? stageMut.to_stage : stageMut.value);
  2789	        if (stageVal) setEditorTripData(prev => {
  2790	          if (!prev || !prev.days) return prev;
  2791	          return { ...prev, days: prev.days.map(d => Number(d.day) === Number(dayNum) ? { ...d, stage: stageVal } : d) };
  2792	        });
  2793	      }
  2794	      // Post-save budget recompute (M10, M25)
  2795	      recomputeBudget(dayNum);
  2796	    } catch (_) {
  2797	      if (saveSeqRef.current !== seq) return;
  2798	      setSaveState('error');
  2799	      setSaveStatusText('error');
  2800	    }
  2801	  }, [isOffline, editorSession, recomputeBudget]);

  2803	  // setEditorSelection bridge (also used internally)

  2809	  // Expose legacy bridge for compatibility (deps: publishedDay.day, saveMutations)
  2810	  useEffect(() => {
  2811	    // Bug3 fix (commit 14f72e30): when accommodation selection changes, push the previously-selected
  2812	    // PLAN hotel back into editorDay.accommodation.options[] so it reappears in the Candidates panel.
  2813	    // The PLAN hotel is absent from options[] because options[] only contains v2 API candidates.
  2814	    const preserveCurrentAccommodationOption = () => {
  2815	      if (!editorDay || !editorDay.accommodation) return;
  2816	      const dayNum = publishedDay && publishedDay.day;
  2817	      if (!dayNum) return;
  2818	      const accKey = dayNum + ':accommodation';

  2820	        ? (editorSelections || {})[accKey]

  2849	    const bridge = (slotId, optionId, originSlotId = null) => {
  2850	      const dayNum = publishedDay && publishedDay.day;

  2864	          if (!dayEntry || !dayEntry.slots) return prev;
  2865	          const tgtSlot = dayEntry.slots[slotId];
  2866	          if (!tgtSlot) return prev;

  2871	            if (srcSlot && srcSlot.options) {
  2872	              const srcOpt = srcSlot.options.find(o => o.option_id === optionId);
  2873	              if (srcOpt) tgtSlot.options.push({ ...srcOpt });
  2874	            }
  2875	          }
  2876	          return updated;
  2877	        });
  2878	      }

  2880	    };
  2881	    window.setEditorSelection = bridge;
  2882	    // applyEditorSelection: used by slot-drop onClick for tap-to-select second step (AC14)
  2883	    // S2 v3 — mirror the bridge cross-meal option-object copy so R1 lookup succeeds.
  2884	    const applyBridge = (optionId, targetSlotId, originSlotId) => {
  2885	      const dayNum = publishedDay && publishedDay.day;

  2892	          if (!prev || !prev.days) return prev;

  2894	          const dayEntry = updated.days && updated.days.find(d => Number(d.day) === Number(dayNum));
  2895	          if (!dayEntry || !dayEntry.slots) return prev;
  2896	          const tgtSlot = dayEntry.slots[targetSlotId];
  2897	          if (!tgtSlot) return prev;
  2898	          if (!tgtSlot.options) tgtSlot.options = [];
  2899	          const alreadyThere = tgtSlot.options.some(o => o.option_id === optionId);
  2900	          if (!alreadyThere) {
  2901	            const srcSlot = dayEntry.slots[originSlotId];
  2902	            if (srcSlot && srcSlot.options) {
  2903	              const srcOpt = srcSlot.options.find(o => o.option_id === optionId);
  2904	              if (srcOpt) tgtSlot.options.push({ ...srcOpt });
  2905	            }
  2906	          }
  2907	          return updated;
  2908	        });
  2909	      }

  2911	      setPendingSelection(null);
  2912	    };
  2913	    window.applyEditorSelection = applyBridge;

  2916	      if (window.applyEditorSelection === applyBridge) delete window.applyEditorSelection;
  2917	    };

  2809	  // Expose legacy bridge for compatibility (deps: publishedDay.day, saveMutations)
  2810	  useEffect(() => {
  2811	    // Bug3 fix (commit 14f72e30): when accommodation selection changes, push the previously-selected
  2812	    // PLAN hotel back into editorDay.accommodation.options[] so it reappears in the Candidates panel.
  2813	    // The PLAN hotel is absent from options[] because options[] only contains v2 API candidates.
  2814	    const preserveCurrentAccommodationOption = () => {
  2815	      if (!editorDay || !editorDay.accommodation) return;
  2816	      const dayNum = publishedDay && publishedDay.day;
  2817	      if (!dayNum) return;
  2818	      const accKey = dayNum + ':accommodation';
  2819	      const selectedAccId = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(editorSelections || {}, accKey)
  2820	        ? (editorSelections || {})[accKey]
  2821	        : editorDay.accommodation.selected_option_id;
  2822	      if (!selectedAccId) return;
  2823	      const options = editorDay.accommodation.options || [];
  2824	      if (options.some(o => o.option_id === selectedAccId)) return; // already in candidates, no-op
  2825	      // Current selection is a PLAN hotel not in options[]; synthesize a v2-shaped option from PLAN data
  2826	      const planAcc = (publishedDay && publishedDay.accommodation) || {};
  2827	      const synth = {
  2828	        option_id: selectedAccId,
  2829	        name: planAcc.name_base || planAcc.name || selectedAccId,
  2830	        name_local: planAcc.name_local || '',
  2831	        location_summary: planAcc.location_base || '',
  2832	        cover_image: planAcc.image || null,
  2833	        image: planAcc.image || null,
  2834	        cost: planAcc.cost || 0
  2835	      };
  2836	      setEditorTripData(prev => {
  2837	        if (!prev || !prev.days) return prev;
  2838	        const updated = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prev));
  2839	        const dayEntry = updated.days.find(d => Number(d.day) === Number(dayNum));
  2840	        if (!dayEntry || !dayEntry.accommodation) return prev;
  2841	        if (!dayEntry.accommodation.options) dayEntry.accommodation.options = [];
  2842	        if (!dayEntry.accommodation.options.some(o => o.option_id === selectedAccId)) {
  2843	          dayEntry.accommodation.options.push(synth);
  2844	        }
  2845	        return updated;
  2846	      });
  2847	    };
  2848	
  2849	    const bridge = (slotId, optionId, originSlotId = null) => {
  2850	      const dayNum = publishedDay && publishedDay.day;
  2851	      if (!dayNum) return;
  2852	      if (slotId === 'accommodation') preserveCurrentAccommodationOption();
  2853	      const updates = { [dayNum + ':' + slotId]: optionId };
  2854	      if (originSlotId && _isCompatible(originSlotId, slotId)) {
  2855	        updates[dayNum + ':' + originSlotId] = optionId;
  2856	      }
  2857	      setEditorSelections(prev => ({ ...prev, ...updates }));
  2858	      // AC20: if cross-meal drop, copy option object into editorTripData so mergeEditorSelectionsIntoPublishedDay can find it in the target slot
  2859	      if (originSlotId && editorDay && editorDay.slots) {
  2860	        setEditorTripData(prev => {
  2861	          if (!prev || !prev.days) return prev;
  2862	          const updated = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prev));
  2863	          const dayEntry = updated.days && updated.days.find(d => Number(d.day) === Number(dayNum));
  2864	          if (!dayEntry || !dayEntry.slots) return prev;
  2865	          const tgtSlot = dayEntry.slots[slotId];
  2866	          if (!tgtSlot) return prev;
  2867	          if (!tgtSlot.options) tgtSlot.options = [];
  2868	          const alreadyThere = tgtSlot.options.some(o => o.option_id === optionId);
  2869	          if (!alreadyThere) {
  2870	            const srcSlot = dayEntry.slots[originSlotId];
  2871	            if (srcSlot && srcSlot.options) {
  2872	              const srcOpt = srcSlot.options.find(o => o.option_id === optionId);
  2873	              if (srcOpt) tgtSlot.options.push({ ...srcOpt });
  2874	            }
  2875	          }
  2876	          return updated;
  2877	        });
  2878	      }
  2879	      saveMutations(dayNum, [{ type: 'select', slot: slotId, option_id: optionId, origin_slot_id: originSlotId }]);
  2880	    };
  2881	    window.setEditorSelection = bridge;
  2882	    // applyEditorSelection: used by slot-drop onClick for tap-to-select second step (AC14)
  2883	    // S2 v3 — mirror the bridge cross-meal option-object copy so R1 lookup succeeds.
  2884	    const applyBridge = (optionId, targetSlotId, originSlotId) => {
  2885	      const dayNum = publishedDay && publishedDay.day;
  2886	      if (!dayNum) return;
  2887	      if (targetSlotId === 'accommodation') preserveCurrentAccommodationOption();

  2889	      setEditorSelections(prev => ({ ...prev, [key]: optionId }));

  2891	        setEditorTripData(prev => {
  2892	          if (!prev || !prev.days) return prev;
  2893	          const updated = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prev));

  2895	          if (!dayEntry || !dayEntry.slots) return prev;
  2896	          const tgtSlot = dayEntry.slots[targetSlotId];
  2897	          if (!tgtSlot) return prev;
  2898	          if (!tgtSlot.options) tgtSlot.options = [];
  2899	          const alreadyThere = tgtSlot.options.some(o => o.option_id === optionId);

   238	    notes_base = (notes_base.rstrip() + "\n\n**Shops:**\n" + bt_base
   239	                  if notes_base else "**Shops:**\n" + bt_base)
   240	    notes_local = (notes_local.rstrip() + "\n\n**推荐店铺：**\n" + bt_local
   241	                   if notes_local else "**推荐店铺：**\n" + bt_local)
   242	    return notes_base, notes_local
   243	
   244	
   245	def _shop_card(gen, item, day_timeline):
   246	    """Build one rendered shopping card."""
   247	    cost = _to_display_cost(gen, item)
   248	    notes_base = item.get("notes_base", "")
   249	    notes_local = item.get("notes_local", "")
   250	    brands = item.get("brands", []) or gen._parse_shopping_brands(notes_base, notes_local)
   251	    notes_base, notes_local = _shop_notes_with_brands(notes_base, notes_local, brands)
   252	    card = _common_fields(item, cost)
   253	    card["notes_base"] = notes_base
   254	    card["notes_local"] = notes_local
   255	    nb, nl = item.get("name_base", ""), item.get("name_local", "")
   256	    mall_image = gen._get_placeholder_image(
   257	        "attraction", poi_name=nl if nl else nb, name_base=nb, name_local=nl)
   258	    mall_time = gen._get_timeline_time(nb, nl, day_timeline, item.get("gaode_id", ""))
   259	    card.update({"type_base": item.get("type_base", ""),
   260	                 "type_local": item.get("type_local", ""),
   261	                 "image": mall_image, "time": mall_time, "links": {}})
   262	    return card
   263	
   264	
   265	def merge_shopping(gen, merged, day_num, day_timeline):
   266	    """Merge shopping.json into merged-dict."""
   267	    if not (gen.shopping and "days" in gen.shopping):
   268	        return
   269	    day_shop = next((d for d in gen.shopping["days"] if d.get("day") == day_num), {})
   270	    for item in day_shop.get("shopping", []) or []:
   271	        card = _shop_card(gen, item, day_timeline)
   272	        merged["shopping"].append(card)
   273	        merged["budget"]["shopping"] += card["cost"]
   274	
   275	
   276	def _acc_card_extra(gen, acc, acc_time):
   277	    """Fields specific to accommodation cards."""
   278	    return {
   279	        "type_base": gen._format_type(acc.get("type_base", "")),
   280	        "type_local": acc.get("type_local", ""),
   281	        "stars": acc.get("stars", 0) or 0,
   282	        "amenities_base": acc.get("amenities_base", []),
   283	        "amenities_local": acc.get("amenities_local", []),
   284	        "check_in": acc.get("check_in", ""),
   285	        "check_out": acc.get("check_out", ""),
   286	        "time": acc_time,
   287	        "image": _image_for(gen, "accommodation", acc),
   288	    }
   289	
   290	
   291	def _acc_card(gen, acc, day_timeline):
   292	    """Build the accommodation card from accommodation.json entry."""
   293	    cost = _to_display_cost(gen, acc)
   294	    card = _common_fields(acc, cost)
   295	    nb, nl = acc.get("name_base", ""), acc.get("name_local", "")
   296	    acc_time = gen._get_timeline_time(nb, nl, day_timeline, acc.get("gaode_id", ""))
   297	    if not acc_time and acc.get("check_in"):
   298	        acc_time = gen._normalize_time(acc.get("check_in"), default_duration_hours=0.5)
   299	    card.update(_acc_card_extra(gen, acc, acc_time))
   300	    return card
   301	
   302	
   303	def merge_accommodation(gen, merged, day_num, day_timeline):
   304	    """Merge accommodation.json into merged-dict."""
   305	    if not (gen.accommodation and "days" in gen.accommodation):
   306	        return
   307	    day_acc = next((d for d in gen.accommodation["days"] if d.get("day") == day_num), {})
   308	    if "accommodation" not in day_acc:
   309	        return
   310	    card = _acc_card(gen, day_acc["accommodation"], day_timeline)
   311	    merged["accommodation"] = card
   312	    merged["budget"]["accommodation"] = card["cost"]
   313	
   314	
   315	def _trans_icon_type(loc_change, ui_local):
   316	    """Pick icon + display strings for an itinerary transportation entry."""
   317	    tt = loc_change.get("type_base", "")
   318	    if "train" in tt.lower():
   319	        return ("\U0001f684", tt or "High-speed Train",
   320	                loc_change.get("type_local", "") or ui_local.get("high_speed_train", ""))
   321	    if "flight" in tt.lower():
   322	        return ("✈️", tt or "Flight",
   323	                loc_change.get("type_local", "") or ui_local.get("flight", ""))
   324	    return "\U0001f68c", tt, loc_change.get("type_local", "")
   325

Findings:

1. BLOCKING — AC2/AC6 interaction flows were not actually verified  
   The requirement is not just `draggable=true` / `data-droppable=true`; it says the user can drag to sidebar to deselect and drop candidates onto slots. QA evidence lists AC1/3/4/5/7, but AC2 and AC6 are only inferred from source.  
   Relevant paths: board payload at `scripts/lib/react_template.tpl:1275-1277`, sidebar clear at `:2364-2367`, accommodation drop handler at `:1322`.  
   PROPOSED_FIX: Run Playwright real interactions: drag accommodation board card to sidebar and verify `/api/save` or persisted JSON has `{slot:"accommodation", option_id:null}`; drag an accommodation candidate onto the board slot and verify new `option_id` saved and rendered.

2. MAJOR — QA does not exercise the changed null-guard branch  
   Day 2 with `editorDay.accommodation` loaded likely passes even before this fix: old `selectedAccId = edAcc ? edAcc.selected_option_id : null` and old `isGated = !edAcc || ...` both work when `edAcc` is non-null. The tested Day 2 evidence therefore proves current behavior, not the two-line fix’s edAcc-null behavior.  
   PROPOSED_FIX: Add a focused fixture/component test or controlled browser state with `editorDay.accommodation === null` and `editorSelections["day:accommodation"]` non-null, then assert card draggable and slot droppable.

3. SCOPE DRIFT — Activity cards are not a dev under-delivery, but QA wording is loose  
   Valid to say “no change needed” for activity cards if scoped to `editorDay` loaded and slot id non-null: current code already gates cafe/attraction/entertainment with `isSelected && !!slotId` at `scripts/lib/react_template.tpl:952`, `:1040`, `:1125`, matching AC5/R4’s non-null slot condition.  
   PROPOSED_FIX: If AC5 claims all activity categories, explicitly verify selected cafe, attraction, and entertainment cards, not just generic selected cards/meals.

4. Fix 2 regression risk — no in-scope blocker found  
   I do not see a valid scoped state where `edAcc=null` plus non-null `selectedAccId` must remain gated. Skipped/late guards remain preserved when `edAcc` exists at `:1318`.  
   PROPOSED_FIX: No code change; add the null-edAcc regression test above if this branch is part of the claim.

5. Scope mismatch / shadowed `edAcc` — not a logical inconsistency  
   The inner `edAcc` at `scripts/lib/react_template.tpl:1317` is recomputed from the same render props as outer `edAcc` at `:1265`; no divergent source.

   PROPOSED_FIX: Cover this through AC2: after sidebar drop, assert the card is no longer draggable/selected and mutation saved `option_id:null`.
